Fork release producer, source ancestry and artifact provenance; guard/deployment configuration; prohibited-file policy. Both database backends; legacy rebuild and restore safety; account-generation and credential provenance; no loss of fork bookkeeping. Exact-account/model fail-closed routing, native quota semantics, physical attempt and continuation ownership, cancellation, exactly-once accounting and default-off new features.&quot;,&quot;rationale&quot;:&quot;Preserve the upstream intent through the fork-owned contracts; evidence covers the composed behavior rather than assuming a clean merge is sufficient.&quot;,&quot;refreshedMainEvidence&quot;:[&quot;refreshed-main&quot;],&quot;reviewer&quot;:{&quot;identity&quot;:&quot;Codex integration owner with domain audits and independent semantic review&quot;,&quot;notes&quot;:&quot;Accepted only at the final code/test/review checkpoint; review scope and fixes are recorded in the review receipt.&quot;,&quot;status&quot;:&quot;accepted&quot;},&quot;selectedResolution&quot;:&quot;Preserve durable device setup, endpoint/account-generation guards, shared authorization, Window Value and pricing; add supported-account thresholds, transformer controls, free-first manual refresh, reauth deadline indicators and alerts. Adopt upstream Claude client fallback 2.1.272 while keeping fork Git-SHA provenance and package release versions. Merge relevant upstream documentation, contributor credit and changelog deduplication while preserving fork routing descriptions, release provenance, manual source-integration and deployment separation. Retain fork SQLite/PostgreSQL schemas, reset-write generations, durable routing-attempt rows and credential-paired state; compose six upstream account fields, guarded threshold writes, rebuild/dedup preservation and additive migration behavior. Integrate 5xx recovery within fork physical budgets, attempt authority, bounded replay/drain and awaited cooldown persistence; preserve scope-aware confirmed quota holds, hosted-dispatch veto and physical accounting. Retry and wire aliases require explicit true opt-ins; authenticated probes remain exact-account.&quot;,&quot;upstreamIntent&quot;:&quot;feat(accounts): pause an account at a chosen percentage of its usage window (0c6d8aafdb8cc270dab6df0086c2d4bac9b909ae)&quot;}</pre>

## `commit:30beeb10045f8436f9c2bd573958b9a5f0e7faf0`

Kind: `upstream-commit`

<pre data-upstream-sync-item="v1">{&quot;dependencies&quot;:[],&quot;disposition&quot;:&quot;semantically-remapped&quot;,&quot;evidence&quot;:{&quot;acceptanceComplete&quot;:{&quot;references&quot;:[&quot;contract-api-ui&quot;,&quot;contract-controls&quot;,&quot;contract-docs-operations&quot;,&quot;contract-persistence&quot;,&quot;contract-routing&quot;,&quot;full-tests&quot;,&quot;database-rehearsal&quot;,&quot;independent-review&quot;,&quot;source-pin&quot;],&quot;status&quot;:&quot;complete&quot;},&quot;combinedDiff&quot;:{&quot;references&quot;:[&quot;combined-review&quot;],&quot;status&quot;:&quot;not-applicable&quot;},&quot;focusedPacket&quot;:{&quot;references&quot;:[&quot;contract-api-ui&quot;,&quot;contract-controls&quot;,&quot;contract-docs-operations&quot;,&quot;contract-persistence&quot;,&quot;contract-routing&quot;,&quot;full-tests&quot;,&quot;database-rehearsal&quot;],&quot;status&quot;:&quot;passed&quot;}},&quot;id&quot;:&quot;commit:30beeb10045f8436f9c2bd573958b9a5f0e7faf0&quot;,&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;upstream-commit&quot;,&quot;protectedForkBehavior&quot;:&quot;Admin/auth boundaries, terminal/manual pause priority, stale-poll suppression, durable setup identities, Window Value and unknown-versus-zero costs. Existing fork modes, operator controls, lazy initialization, explicit activation and identity/version policy. Fork release producer, source ancestry and artifact provenance; guard/deployment configuration; prohibited-file policy. Both database backends; legacy rebuild and restore safety; account-generation and credential provenance; no loss of fork bookkeeping. Exact-account/model fail-closed routing, native quota semantics, physical attempt and continuation ownership, cancellation, exactly-once accounting and default-off new features.&quot;,&quot;rationale&quot;:&quot;Preserve the upstream intent through the fork-owned contracts; evidence covers the composed behavior rather than assuming a clean merge is sufficient.&quot;,&quot;refreshedMainEvidence&quot;:[&quot;refreshed-main&quot;],&quot;reviewer&quot;:{&quot;identity&quot;:&quot;Codex integration owner with domain audits and independent semantic review&quot;,&quot;notes&quot;:&quot;Accepted only at the final code/test/review checkpoint; review scope and fixes are recorded in the review receipt.&quot;,&quot;status&quot;:&quot;accepted&quot;},&quot;selectedResolution&quot;:&quot;Preserve durable device setup, endpoint/account-generation guards, shared authorization, Window Value and pricing; add supported-account thresholds, transformer controls, free-first manual refresh, reauth deadline indicators and alerts. Add threshold CLI parsing/help and shared account controls while retaining fork lazy CLI initialization, managed routing, mode registry, configuration defaults and no automatic source deployment. Adopt upstream Claude client fallback 2.1.272 while keeping fork Git-SHA provenance and package release versions. Merge relevant upstream documentation, contributor credit and changelog deduplication while preserving fork routing descriptions, release provenance, manual source-integration and deployment separation. Retain fork SQLite/PostgreSQL schemas, reset-write generations, durable routing-attempt rows and credential-paired state; compose six upstream account fields, guarded threshold writes, rebuild/dedup preservation and additive migration behavior. Integrate 5xx recovery within fork physical budgets, attempt authority, bounded replay/drain and awaited cooldown persistence; preserve scope-aware confirmed quota holds, hosted-dispatch veto and physical accounting. Retry and wire aliases require explicit true opt-ins; authenticated probes remain exact-account.&quot;,&quot;upstreamIntent&quot;:&quot;feat(accounts): pause an account at a chosen percentage of its usage window (PR #466) (30beeb10045f8436f9c2bd573958b9a5f0e7faf0)&quot;}</pre>

Single continuation owner, trusted carriers, bounded parsing and replay, ingress-header stripping, unknown-versus-zero quota evidence. Exact-account/model fail-closed routing, native quota semantics, physical attempt and continuation ownership, cancellation, exactly-once accounting and default-off new features.&quot;,&quot;rationale&quot;:&quot;Preserve the upstream intent through the fork-owned contracts; evidence covers the composed behavior rather than assuming a clean merge is sufficient.&quot;,&quot;refreshedMainEvidence&quot;:[&quot;refreshed-main&quot;],&quot;reviewer&quot;:{&quot;identity&quot;:&quot;Codex integration owner with domain audits and independent semantic review&quot;,&quot;notes&quot;:&quot;Accepted only at the final code/test/review checkpoint; review scope and fixes are recorded in the review receipt.&quot;,&quot;status&quot;:&quot;accepted&quot;},&quot;selectedResolution&quot;:&quot;Preserve durable device setup, endpoint/account-generation guards, shared authorization, Window Value and pricing; add supported-account thresholds, transformer controls, free-first manual refresh, reauth deadline indicators and alerts. Compose upstream custom/namespaced tools, native Codex client fields and hardened free usage endpoint with fork continuation ownership, header/admission security, generation fences, canonical usage snapshots and conservative reset evidence. Integrate 5xx recovery within fork physical budgets, attempt authority, bounded replay/drain and awaited cooldown persistence; preserve scope-aware confirmed quota holds, hosted-dispatch veto and physical accounting. Retry and wire aliases require explicit true opt-ins; authenticated probes remain exact-account.&quot;,&quot;upstreamIntent&quot;:&quot;feat: add per-account request transformer for OpenAI-compatible providers (PR #462) (5649ef2e1646fc48b0ec8ee612fca0b9391d2bf2)&quot;}</pre>

Existing fork modes, operator controls, lazy initialization, explicit activation and identity/version policy. Both database backends; legacy rebuild and restore safety; account-generation and credential provenance; no loss of fork bookkeeping. Single continuation owner, trusted carriers, bounded parsing and replay, ingress-header stripping, unknown-versus-zero quota evidence. Exact-account/model fail-closed routing, native quota semantics, physical attempt and continuation ownership, cancellation, exactly-once accounting and default-off new features.&quot;,&quot;rationale&quot;:&quot;Preserve the upstream intent through the fork-owned contracts; evidence covers the composed behavior rather than assuming a clean merge is sufficient.&quot;,&quot;refreshedMainEvidence&quot;:[&quot;refreshed-main&quot;],&quot;reviewer&quot;:{&quot;identity&quot;:&quot;Codex integration owner with domain audits and independent semantic review&quot;,&quot;notes&quot;:&quot;Accepted only at the final code/test/review checkpoint; review scope and fixes are recorded in the review receipt.&quot;,&quot;status&quot;:&quot;accepted&quot;},&quot;selectedResolution&quot;:&quot;Preserve durable device setup, endpoint/account-generation guards, shared authorization, Window Value and pricing; add supported-account thresholds, transformer controls, free-first manual refresh, reauth deadline indicators and alerts. Add threshold CLI parsing/help and shared account controls while retaining fork lazy CLI initialization, managed routing, mode registry, configuration defaults and no automatic source deployment. Adopt upstream Claude client fallback 2.1.272 while keeping fork Git-SHA provenance and package release versions. Retain fork SQLite/PostgreSQL schemas, reset-write generations, durable routing-attempt rows and credential-paired state; compose six upstream account fields, guarded threshold writes, rebuild/dedup preservation and additive migration behavior. Compose upstream custom/namespaced tools, native Codex client fields and hardened free usage endpoint with fork continuation ownership, header/admission security, generation fences, canonical usage snapshots and conservative reset evidence. Integrate 5xx recovery within fork physical budgets, attempt authority, bounded replay/drain and awaited cooldown persistence; preserve scope-aware confirmed quota holds, hosted-dispatch veto and physical accounting. Retry and wire aliases require explicit true opt-ins; authenticated probes remain exact-account.&quot;,&quot;upstreamIntent&quot;:&quot;feat: warn before Claude OAuth accounts hit their manual reauth deadline (8ba103f68de197e4d5317272994e3f72b184a3c9)&quot;}</pre>

Adopt upstream Claude client fallback 2.1.272 while keeping fork Git-SHA provenance and package release versions. Retain fork SQLite/PostgreSQL schemas, reset-write generations, durable routing-attempt rows and credential-paired state; compose six upstream account fields, guarded threshold writes, rebuild/dedup preservation and additive migration behavior. Compose upstream custom/namespaced tools, native Codex client fields and hardened free usage endpoint with fork continuation ownership, header/admission security, generation fences, canonical usage snapshots and conservative reset evidence. Integrate 5xx recovery within fork physical budgets, attempt authority, bounded replay/drain and awaited cooldown persistence; preserve scope-aware confirmed quota holds, hosted-dispatch veto and physical accounting. Retry and wire aliases require explicit true opt-ins; authenticated probes remain exact-account.&quot;,&quot;upstreamIntent&quot;:&quot;feat: warn before Claude OAuth accounts hit their manual reauth deadline (PR #461) (c55d78af84c037f1677e7a13a8b400ccf3c80898)&quot;}</pre>
